, whose order is given by

\displaystyle T^{-\delta/2}\|\mathbf{K}_{13}\|\leq\left\|\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{2}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\mathbf{F}_{+2}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{2,i}^{0}+\mathbf{F}_{1}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{1,i}^{0}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\widehat{\boldsymbol{\gamma}}_{1,i})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{2,i}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{2}^{0\prime}\right\|T^{-\delta/2}\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{2}\|

\displaystyle\leq O(1)N^{-1}T^{-(\nu_{2}+\delta)/2}\|\mathbf{F}_{+2}^{0}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{+2}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{2}^{0}\mathbf{F}_{2}^{0\prime}\|

\displaystyle+O(1)\left\|\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{2}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\mathbf{F}_{1}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{1,i}^{0}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\widehat{\boldsymbol{\gamma}}_{1,i})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{2,i}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{2}^{0\prime}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{P}(N^{-(1-p)}T^{-(\delta-\nu_{3})/2})+\left(\frac{1}{NT^{\delta}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\|\mathbf{F}_{1}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{1,i}^{0}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\widehat{\boldsymbol{\gamma}}_{1,i}\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{1}{NT^{\nu_{2}}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\|\mathbf{F}_{2}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{2,i}^{0}\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}(N^{-1/2}T^{-(\delta-1)/2})+O_{P}(T^{-\delta/2})+O_{P}(T^{q-(\delta+\nu_{1})/2})+O_{P}(N^{-(1-p)}T^{-(\delta-\nu_{2})/2}),

(A.76)

where the second equality follows from Lemma
A.4
and Assumption
1
. The order of
T^{-\delta/2}\|\mathbf{K}_{14}\|
is the same.

For
\mathbf{K}_{15}
,

\displaystyle T^{-\delta/2}\|\mathbf{K}_{15}\|=T^{-\delta/2}\left\|\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{2}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{U}_{i}\mathbf{U}_{i}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{2}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{2}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\|\mathbf{F}_{1}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{1,i}^{0}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{1}\widehat{\boldsymbol{\gamma}}_{1,i}\|^{2}+O_{P}(1)\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{2}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\|\mathbf{F}_{+2}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{+2,i}^{0}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}(T^{-(\nu_{2}+\delta)/2})+O_{P}(N^{-1}T^{1-(\nu_{2}+\delta)/2})+O_{P}(T^{2q-\nu_{1}-(\nu_{2}+\delta)/2})

\displaystyle+O_{P}(N^{-2(1-p)}T^{-(\delta-\nu_{2})/2})+O_{P}(T^{\nu_{3}-(\nu_{2}+\delta)/2}),

(A.77)

where the second equality follows from Lemma
A.4
.

We now insert the above results for
\mathbf{K}_{1},\ldots,\mathbf{K}_{15}
into (
A.62
). But first we left multiply by
T^{-\nu_{2}}\mathbf{F}_{2}^{0\prime}
and
T^{-(\nu_{2}+\delta)/2}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{2}
respectively. It then gives

\displaystyle\|T^{-(\nu_{2}+\delta)/2}\mathbf{F}_{2}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{2}(T^{-\nu_{2}}\mathbf{V}_{2})-(T^{-\nu_{2}}\mathbf{F}_{2}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{2}^{0})(N^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{2}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{2}^{0})(T^{-(\nu_{2}+\delta)/2}\mathbf{F}_{2}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{2})\|

\displaystyle=O_{P}(N^{-1/2}T^{-(\nu_{2}-1)/2})+O_{P}(T^{-\nu_{2}/2})+O_{P}(T^{-(\nu_{2}-\nu_{3})})

\displaystyle+O_{P}(T^{q-(\nu_{1}+\nu_{2})/2})+O_{P}(N^{-(1-p)})

(A.78)

and

\displaystyle\|T^{-\nu_{2}}\mathbf{V}_{2}-(T^{-(\nu_{2}+\delta)/2}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{2}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{2}^{0})(N^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{2}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{2}^{0})(T^{-(\nu_{2}+\delta)/2}\mathbf{F}_{2}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{2})\|

\displaystyle=O_{P}(N^{-1/2}T^{-(\nu_{2}-1)/2})+O_{P}(T^{-\nu_{2}/2})+O_{P}(T^{-(\nu_{2}-\nu_{3})})

\displaystyle+O_{P}(T^{q-(\nu_{1}+\nu_{2})/2})+O_{P}(N^{-(1-p)}).

(A.79)

The rest of the proofs of (a) and (b) follows from the same arguments used in Lemma
A.3
. It is therefore omitted.
\blacksquare

Before continuing onto the proof of Lemma
A.6
, we note that the rates given in Lemma
A.5
can actually be improved upon. Suppose that
d_{2}
in known. Consider the next term which is a part of
\mathbf{K}_{15}
:

\displaystyle N^{-1}T^{-(\nu_{2}/2+\delta})\|\mathbf{F}_{+2}^{0}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{+2}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{+2}^{0}\mathbf{F}_{+2}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{2}\|

\displaystyle=N^{-1}T^{-(\nu_{2}/2+\delta})\|\mathbf{F}_{+2}^{0}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{+2}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{+2}^{0}\mathbf{F}_{+2}^{0\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{0}-\mathbf{F}_{2}^{0}\mathbf{H}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{0}+\mathbf{F}_{2}^{0}\mathbf{H}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{0})(\mathbf{V}_{2}^{0})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq N^{-1}T^{-(\nu_{2}/2+\delta})\|\mathbf{F}_{+2}^{0}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{+2}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{+2}^{0}\mathbf{F}_{+2}^{0\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{0}-\mathbf{F}_{2}^{0}\mathbf{H}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{0})(\mathbf{V}_{2}^{0})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle+N^{-1}T^{-(\nu_{2}/2+\delta})\|\mathbf{F}_{+2}^{0}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{+2}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{+2}^{0}\mathbf{F}_{+2}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{2}^{0}\mathbf{H}_{2}\|

\displaystyle\leq O_{P}(1)T^{\nu_{3}-\nu_{2}}\cdot T^{-(\nu_{2}/2+\delta)}\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{0}-\mathbf{F}_{2}^{0}\mathbf{H}_{2}\mathbf{V}_{2}^{0}\|+O_{P}(1)T^{\nu_{3}/2-(\nu_{2}+\delta/2)}\|\mathbf{F}_{+2}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{2}^{0}\|,

(A.80)

where
\mathbf{V}_{2}^{0}
and
\mathbf{H}_{2}
are defined in Appendix
A.1
. Simple algebra shows that the term
T^{-(\nu_{2}-\nu_{3})}
in
\tau_{NT}
of Lemma
A.5
can be dropped.

Proof of Lemma
A.6
.

Consider (a). Let us assume without loss of generality that
\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}=\mathbf{0}_{d_{x}\times 1}
, as in
Bai 2009
. It then follows that

\displaystyle(NT)^{-1}[\mathrm{SSR}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1},\widehat{\mathbf{F}})-\mathrm{SSR}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0},\widehat{\mathbf{F}})]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(-\mathbf{X}_{i}\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}+\mathbf{F}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i}^{0})^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}(-\mathbf{X}_{i}\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}+\mathbf{F}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i}^{0})

\displaystyle-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}^{\prime}\frac{2}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}+\frac{2}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}+\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(-\mathbf{X}_{i}\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}+\mathbf{F}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i}^{0})^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}(-\mathbf{X}_{i}\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}+\mathbf{F}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i}^{0})

\displaystyle+\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}^{\prime}\frac{2}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}-\frac{2}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}-\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{X}_{i}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})

\displaystyle-2\left(\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{X}_{i}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\right)^{1/2}\left(\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{X}_{i}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{F}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i}^{0}-(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})^{\prime}\frac{2}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}.

(A.81)

Note that by expanding the term
\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{F}^{0}
as in the proof for the second result of this lemma, we can obtain that

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{F}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\|\left\|\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{F}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle=O_{P}(\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\|)[O_{P}((NT)^{-1/2})+o_{P}(\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})\|)].

(A.82)

It follows that

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle\geq(NT)^{-1}[\mathrm{SSR}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1},\widehat{\mathbf{F}})-\mathrm{SSR}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0},\widehat{\mathbf{F}})]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{X}_{i}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})

\displaystyle+O_{P}(\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\|)[O_{P}((NT)^{-1/2})+o_{P}(\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})\|)]

\displaystyle\geq\lambda_{min}\left(\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{D}_{T}\right)\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\|^{2}

\displaystyle+O_{P}(\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\|)[O_{P}((NT)^{-1/2})+o_{P}(\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})\|)],

(A.83)

where the first term on the right is quadratic in
\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\|
. Hence, to ensure the right-hand side is non-positive,
\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\|
cannot converge to zero at a rate faster than
(NT)^{-1/2}\vee\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})\|
. It follows that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\|=O_{P}((NT)^{-1/2}\vee\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})\|),

(A.84)

as was to be shown.

We now turn to (b). Note that

\displaystyle\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{D}_{T}\left(\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{D}_{T}\right)^{-1}

\displaystyle\times\left(\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{F}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i}^{0}+\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{B}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{L}+\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}\right),

(A.85)

with obvious definitions of
\mathbf{L}
and
\mathbf{B}
.

Consider
\mathbf{L}
. Let
\mathbf{Q}_{g}=(N^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0})(T^{-(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g})
, such that
\mathbf{H}_{g}^{-1}=T^{-(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}\mathbf{V}_{g}^{0}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}
. We also introduce
\mathbf{e}_{g,i}
, which is defined to be zero for
g=1
and
\mathbf{e}_{g,i}=\sum_{j=1}^{g-1}(\mathbf{F}_{j}^{0}\gamma_{j,i}^{0}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{j}\widehat{\boldsymbol{\gamma}}_{j,i})
for
g=2,\ldots,G
. In this notation,

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{F}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i}^{0}=-\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{H}_{g}^{-1}-\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}

\displaystyle=-(\mathbf{L}_{1}+\cdots+\mathbf{L}_{15}),

(A.86)

where

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{X}_{j}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0},

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{X}_{j}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,j}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0},

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}_{3}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,j}^{0}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0},

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}_{4}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{X}_{j}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\mathbf{e}_{g,j}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0},

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}_{5}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{e}_{g,j}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0},

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}_{6}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{X}_{j}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0},

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}_{7}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0},

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}_{8}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0},

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}_{9}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,j}^{0}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0},

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}_{10}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,j}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0},

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}_{11}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}\mathbf{e}_{g,j}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0},

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}_{12}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{e}_{g,j}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0},

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}_{13}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{e}_{g,j}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,j}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0},

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}_{14}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,j}^{0}\mathbf{e}_{g,j}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0},

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}_{15}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{e}_{g,j}\mathbf{e}_{g,j}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}.

We now evaluate each of these terms. From the analysis of
\mathbf{L}_{2}
below, it is easy to show that
\|\mathbf{L}_{1}\|=o_{P}(\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})\|)
. We therefore start from
\mathbf{L}_{2}
, which we write as

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})(\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,j}^{0})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N^{2}T}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{D}_{T}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,j}^{0\prime}(T^{-(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g})(T^{-(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g})^{-1}

\displaystyle\times(N^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0})^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{D}_{T}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,j}^{0\prime}(\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0})^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{D}_{T}a_{ji}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}).

(A.87)

We will use this expression for
\mathbf{L}_{2}
later.

Let us now move on to
\mathbf{L}_{3}
.

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}_{3}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,j}^{0}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\sum_{g=1}^{G}(\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{H}_{g}^{-1})

\displaystyle\times\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,j}^{0}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0},

(A.88)

where, by using arguments that are similar to those used in the proofs of Lemmas
A.3
and
A.5
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\sum_{g=1}^{G}(\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{H}_{g}^{-1})\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,j}^{0}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left(\sum_{g=1}^{G}\|\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{H}_{g}^{-1}\|\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\|\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,j}^{0}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})^{\prime}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\|\|\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}\|\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq O_{P}(1)\sum_{g=1}^{G}T^{-\nu_{g}}\|\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{H}_{g}^{-1}\|^{2}\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\|^{2}

\displaystyle=o_{P}(\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\|^{2})

(A.89)

implying

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{L}_{3}\|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\|\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\times\left(\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\sum_{g=1}^{G}(\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{H}_{g}^{-1})\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,j}^{0}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})^{\prime}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}\right\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle=o_{P}(\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\|).

(A.90)

The same steps can be used to show that
\mathbf{L}_{4}
and
\mathbf{L}_{5}
are of the same order.

For
\mathbf{L}_{6}
, write

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}_{6}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{X}_{j}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{N^{2}T^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2+1}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{X}_{j}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}\mathbf{H}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}

\displaystyle+\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{N^{2}T^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2+1}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{X}_{j}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}-\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}\mathbf{H}_{g})\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0},

(A.91)

where the first term on the right is bounded by

\displaystyle\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{N^{2}T^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2+1}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\|\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{D}_{T}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}\mathbf{H}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}\|\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\|

\displaystyle\leq O_{P}(1)\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\|\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{N^{2}T^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2+1}}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\|\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\|\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\times\left(\sum_{j=1}^{N}\|\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{D}_{T}\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{N}\|\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}\|^{2}\right)^{1/2}\|\mathbf{H}_{g}\|

\displaystyle\leq O_{P}(1)\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\|\sum_{g=1}^{G}T^{-(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2-1}O_{P}(\sqrt{T})O_{P}(\sqrt{T})O_{P}(T^{\nu_{g}/2})O_{P}(T^{-(\nu_{g}-\delta)/2})

\displaystyle=o_{P}(\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\|).

(A.92)

The second term is of the same order. Thus,
\|\mathbf{L}_{6}\|=o_{P}(\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\|)
. The same is true for
\|\mathbf{L}_{7}\|
.

We now examine
\mathbf{L}_{8}
. Let us define
\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon}=N^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon,i}
, in which
\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon,i}
has been defined in Assumption
3
.
\mathbf{L}_{8}
can then be written as

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}_{8}

\displaystyle=\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2+1}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}

\displaystyle+\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{N^{2}T^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2+1}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}(\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}^{\prime}-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon,j})\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}

\displaystyle-\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{N^{2}T^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2+1}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{F}}(\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}^{\prime}-\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon,j})\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}.

(A.93)

For the first term on the right,

\displaystyle\sqrt{NT}\left\|\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2+1}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{\sqrt{N}T^{(\nu_{g}+\delta+1)/2}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\|\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon,j}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}\|

\displaystyle\leq O_{P}(1)\sum_{g=1}^{G}N^{-1/2}T^{-(\nu_{g}+\delta+1)/2}O_{P}(NT)O_{P}(T^{(\delta-1)/2})

\displaystyle\leq O_{P}(1)\sum_{g=1}^{G}N^{1/2}T^{-\nu_{g}/2}=O_{P}(\sqrt{N}T^{-\nu_{G}/2})=O_{P}(1),

(A.94)

where the last equality holds by
NT^{-\nu_{G}}<\infty
. Let

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbf{A}}_{1}=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{F_{0}}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon}T^{-(\nu_{G}-1)/2}\mathbf{F}_{G}^{0}(T^{-\nu_{G}}\mathbf{F}_{G}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{G}^{0})^{-1}(N^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{G}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{G}^{0})^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{G,i}^{0}.

(A.95)

Note how
\mathbf{A}_{1}=\plim_{N,T\to\infty}E(\overline{\mathbf{A}}_{1}|\mathcal{C})
. In this notation,

\displaystyle\sqrt{NT}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2+1}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{NT}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2+1}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{F_{0}}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}(T^{-(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g})^{-1}(N^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0})^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}(1+o_{P}(1))

\displaystyle=\sum_{g=1}^{G}\sqrt{\frac{N}{T^{\nu_{g}}}}\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{F_{0}}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon}T^{-(\nu_{g}-1)/2}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}(T^{-\nu_{g}}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0})^{-1}(N^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0})^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}(1+o_{P}(1))

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{N}{T^{\nu_{G}}}}\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{F_{0}}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon}T^{-(\nu_{G}-1)/2}\mathbf{F}_{G}^{0}(T^{-\nu_{G}}\mathbf{F}_{G}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{G}^{0})^{-1}(N^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{G}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{G}^{0})^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{G,i}^{0}(1+o_{P}(1))

\displaystyle=\sqrt{N}T^{-\nu_{G}/2}\overline{\mathbf{A}}_{1}(1+o_{P}(1)),

(A.96)

where the second equality follows from arguments similar to those used in (
A.30
). Note also that
\lim\sqrt{N}T^{-\nu_{G}/2}<\infty
. Further use of the same steps used by
Jiang et al. 2020
establishes that the second and third terms of
\mathbf{L}_{8}
are
o_{P}(\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})\|)+o_{P}((NT)^{-1/2})
. Hence,

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}_{8}=T^{-(\nu_{G}+1)/2}\overline{\mathbf{A}}_{1}+o_{P}(\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})\|)+o_{P}((NT)^{-1/2}).

(A.97)

\mathbf{L}_{9}
can be written as

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}_{9}

\displaystyle=\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{N^{2}T^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2+1}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}(\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{H}_{g}^{-1})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,j}^{0}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}\mathbf{H}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}

\displaystyle+\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{N^{2}T^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2+1}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}(\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{H}_{g}^{-1})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,j}^{0}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}-\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}\mathbf{H}_{g})\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0},

(A.98)

where the first term on the right-hand side is bounded by

\displaystyle\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{N^{2}T^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2+1}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\|\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\|\|\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{H}_{g}^{-1}\|\|T^{-(\nu_{g}-1)/2}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}\|

\displaystyle\times T^{(\nu_{g}-1)/2}\|\mathbf{H}_{g}\|\|\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}\|

\displaystyle\leq O_{P}(1)\sum_{g=1}^{G}N^{-1}T^{-(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2-1}O_{P}(\sqrt{T})\|\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{H}_{g}^{-1}\|O_{P}(\sqrt{NT})O_{P}(T^{(\nu_{g}-1)/2})O_{P}(T^{-(\nu_{g}-\delta)/2})

\displaystyle\leq O_{P}(1)\sum_{g=1}^{G}(NT)^{-1/2}T^{-\nu_{g}/2}\|\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{H}_{g}^{-1}\|

\displaystyle=o_{P}((NT)^{-1/2}),

(A.99)

where the first inequality follows from
\|T^{-(\nu_{g}-1)/2}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}\|=O_{P}(\sqrt{NT})
. The second term on the right-hand side of
\mathbf{L}_{9}
is also
o_{P}((NT)^{-1/2})
. We therefore conclude that
\|\mathbf{L}_{9}\|=o_{P}((NT)^{-1/2})
.

\mathbf{L}_{10}
can be written more compactly as

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}_{10}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{g=1}^{G}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,j}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}a_{ij},

(A.100)

which we will again make use of later.

Let us move on to
\mathbf{L}_{11}
. We begin by rewriting
\mathbf{e}_{g,j}
as

\displaystyle\mathbf{e}_{g,j}

\displaystyle=\sum_{d=1}^{g-1}[\mathbf{F}_{d}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{d,j}^{0}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{d}T^{-\delta}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{d}^{\prime}(\mathbf{y}_{j}-\mathbf{X}_{j}\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{d}\widehat{\boldsymbol{\gamma}}_{d,j})]

\displaystyle=\sum_{d=1}^{g-1}[-\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{F}_{d}}\mathbf{X}_{j}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})+\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}_{d}}\mathbf{F}_{d}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{d,j}^{0}-\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{F}_{d}}\mathbf{F}_{+d}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{+d,j}^{0}-\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{F}_{d}}\mathbf{e}_{d,j}-\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{F}_{d}}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}],

(A.101)

where
\mathbf{F}_{+d}^{0}=(\mathbf{F}_{d+1}^{0},\ldots,\mathbf{F}_{G}^{0})
and
\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{+d,j}^{0}=(\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{d+1,j}^{0\prime},\ldots,\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{G,j}^{0\prime})^{\prime}
, as in Appendix
A.1
. By inserting this into
\mathbf{L}_{11}
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}_{11}

\displaystyle=\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}\sum_{d=1}^{g-1}[-\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{F}_{d}}\mathbf{X}_{j}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})

\displaystyle+\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}_{d}}\mathbf{F}_{d}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{d,j}^{0}-\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{F}_{d}}\mathbf{F}_{+d}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{+d,j}^{0}-\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{F}_{d}}\mathbf{e}_{d,j}-\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{F}_{d}}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}]^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}.

(A.102)

Hence,
\mathbf{L}_{11}
can be written as a sum of five terms. There is no need to study the forth term, the one due to
\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{F}_{d}}\mathbf{e}_{d,j}
, as we can keep expanding
\mathbf{e}_{d,j}
until we cannot. The first and fifth terms are
o_{P}(\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})\|)
and
o_{P}((NT)^{-1/2})
, respectively, by the same arguments used for evaluating
\mathbf{L}_{7}
and
\mathbf{L}_{8}
. Moreover, the steps used for evaluating
\mathbf{L}_{9}
can be used to show that

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}\sum_{d=1}^{g-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{d,j}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{F}_{d}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}_{d}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{d=1}^{g-1}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{d}^{0}\mathbf{F}_{d}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}_{d}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{N^{2}T^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2+1}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{d=1}^{g-1}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{d}^{0}(\mathbf{F}_{d}^{0}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{d}\mathbf{H}_{d}^{-1})^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}_{d}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle+\left\|\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{N^{2}T^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2+1}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{d=1}^{g-1}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{F}}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{d}^{0}(\mathbf{F}_{d}^{0}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{d}\mathbf{H}_{d}^{-1})^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}_{d}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle=o_{P}((NT)^{-1/2}).

(A.103)

The second term in
\mathbf{L}_{11}
is therefore negligible. It remains to consider the third term, which is

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}\sum_{d=1}^{g-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{+d,j}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{+d}^{0\prime}\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{F}_{d}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{d=1}^{g-1}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{+d}^{0}\mathbf{F}_{+d}^{0\prime}\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{F}_{d}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle=o_{P}(\|\mathbf{L}_{10}\|)=o_{P}((NT)^{-1/2})

(A.104)

where we have used Assumption
5
. Therefore,
\|\mathbf{L}_{11}\|=o_{P}((NT)^{-1/2})
.

For
\mathbf{L}_{12}
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}_{12}

\displaystyle=\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{e}_{g,j}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}

\displaystyle=\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i}^{0}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}=\mathbf{L}_{9},

(A.105)

where the second equality follows from the construction of
\mathbf{e}_{g,j}
. Hence, since
\mathbf{L}_{9}
is negligible,
\mathbf{L}_{12}
is also negligible.

Next up is
\mathbf{L}_{13}
, whose order can be worked out in the following way:

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{L}_{13}\|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{N^{2}T}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\sum_{d=1}^{g-1}\mathbf{F}_{d}^{0}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{d}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0}(N^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0})^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{N^{2}T}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\left\|\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\sum_{d=1}^{g-1}(\mathbf{F}_{d}^{0}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{d}\mathbf{H}_{d}^{-1})\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{d}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0}(N^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0})^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq O_{P}(1)\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{N^{2}T}\sum_{d=1}^{g-1}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\|\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\|\|\mathbf{F}_{d}^{0}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{d}\mathbf{H}_{d}^{-1}\|\|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{d}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0}\|\|\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}\|

\displaystyle\leq O_{P}(1)\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{d=1}^{g-1}O_{P}(\sqrt{T})\|\mathbf{F}_{d}^{0}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{d}\mathbf{H}_{d}^{-1}\|O_{P}(N^{p})

\displaystyle\leq O_{P}(1)\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{N^{1-p}\sqrt{T}}\sum_{d=1}^{g-1}\|\mathbf{F}_{d}^{0}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{d}\mathbf{H}_{d}^{-1}\|=o_{P}((NT)^{-1/2}),

(A.106)

where the first inequality follows from the construction of
\mathbf{e}_{g,j}
, and the last equality follows by going through a development similar to the first result of Lemma
A.5
and further using a development similar to (
A.6
). The same arguments show that
\|\mathbf{L}_{14}\|
and
\|\mathbf{L}_{15}\|
are negligible, too.

We now put everything together. This yields

\displaystyle\mathbf{L}

\displaystyle=-(\mathbf{L}_{1}+\cdots+\mathbf{L}_{15})

\displaystyle=-\mathbf{L}_{2}-\mathbf{L}_{8}-\mathbf{L}_{10}+o_{P}(\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})\|)+o_{P}((NT)^{-1/2})

\displaystyle=\left(-\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{D}_{T}a_{ji}+o_{P}(1)\right)\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})-T^{(\nu_{G}+1)/2}\overline{\mathbf{A}}_{1}

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}a_{ji}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}+o_{P}((NT)^{-1/2}),

(A.107)

which in turn implies

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})

\displaystyle=\mathbf{B}^{-1}\left(\mathbf{L}+\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}\right)

\displaystyle=(\mathbf{B}^{-1}\mathbf{N}+o_{P}(1))\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})+\mathbf{B}^{-1}\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle-T^{-(\nu_{G}+1)/2}\mathbf{B}^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{A}}_{1}+o_{P}((NT)^{-1/2}),

(A.108)

where
\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\mathbf{F})
is defined in Assumption
2
, and

\displaystyle\mathbf{N}=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{D}_{T}a_{ij}.

This expression for
\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})
can be inserted into
\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})
, giving

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})

\displaystyle=\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})-\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})

\displaystyle=(\mathbf{B}^{-1}\mathbf{N}+o_{P}(1))\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})+\mathbf{B}^{-1}\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle-\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})-T^{-(\nu_{G}+1)/2}\mathbf{B}^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{A}}_{1}+o_{P}((NT)^{-1/2}),

(A.109)

which can be solved for
\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})

\displaystyle=\mathbf{B}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}-\mathbf{B}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})-T^{-(\nu_{G}+1)/2}\overline{\mathbf{A}}_{1}\right)

\displaystyle+o_{P}(\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\|)+o_{P}((NT)^{-1/2}).

(A.110)

Also, making use of Assumption
3
, it is not difficult to show that
(NT)^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}=O_{P}((NT)^{-1/2})
. Hence,

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})+\mathbf{B}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{-1}\mathbf{B}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})

\displaystyle=\mathbf{B}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{-1}\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}-T^{-(\nu_{G}+1)/2}\mathbf{B}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{A}}_{1}

\displaystyle+o_{P}(\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\|)+o_{P}((NT)^{-1/2}).

(A.111)

By using the fact that

\displaystyle\mathbf{B}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{-1}\mathbf{B}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}=\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{\prime}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})\right)^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{X}_{i},

(A.112)

the left-hand side of this last equation can be written as

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})+\mathbf{B}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{-1}\mathbf{B}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})

\displaystyle=\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\left[\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}+\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{\prime}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})\right)^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{X}_{i}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\right].

(A.113)

It follows that

\displaystyle\sqrt{NT}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\left[\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}+\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{\prime}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})\right)^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{X}_{i}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{B}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{-1}\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}-\sqrt{N}T^{-\nu_{G}/2}\mathbf{B}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{A}}_{1}

\displaystyle+o_{P}(\sqrt{NT}\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\|)+o_{P}(1).

(A.114)

Consider the
o_{P}(\sqrt{NT}\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\|)
reminder term. By the first result of this lemma,
\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\|=O_{P}((NT)^{-1/2}\vee\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})\|)
. This can be inserted into (
A.6
), giving

\displaystyle\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})

\displaystyle=\mathbf{B}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{-1}\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}+o_{P}((NT)^{-1/2}\vee\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})\|)

\displaystyle=O_{P}((NT)^{-1/2})+o_{P}(\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})\|),

(A.115)

which in turn implies

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})\|=O_{P}((NT)^{-1/2}).

(A.116)

The second result then follows.
\blacksquare

A.7
Proofs of main results

Proof of Lemma
3.1
.

Without loss of generality, we assume that
\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}=\mathbf{0}_{d_{x}\times 1}
. This implies

\displaystyle(NT)^{-1}[\mathrm{SSR}(\boldsymbol{\beta},\mathbf{F})-\mathrm{SSR}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0},\mathbf{F}^{0})]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\mathbf{X}_{i}\boldsymbol{\beta}+\mathbf{F}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i}^{0})^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{F}(\mathbf{X}_{i}\boldsymbol{\beta}+\mathbf{F}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i}^{0})

\displaystyle+\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\prime}\frac{2}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{F}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}+\frac{2}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{F}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}+\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}^{\prime}(\mathbf{P}_{F^{0}}-\mathbf{P}_{F})\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\beta}+\mathbf{F}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i}^{0})^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{F}(\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\beta}+\mathbf{F}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i}^{0})

\displaystyle+\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\prime}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\frac{2}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{F}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}+\frac{2}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{F}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}+O_{P}(N^{-1}\vee T^{-1})

\displaystyle=\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\prime}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\mathbf{B}(\mathbf{F})\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\beta}+\boldsymbol{\theta}^{\prime}\mathbf{B}\boldsymbol{\theta}+\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\prime}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\frac{2}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{F}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{F}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}+O_{P}(N^{-1}\vee T^{-1}),

(A.1)

where the second equality follows from Lemma
A.2
, and
\boldsymbol{\theta}=\boldsymbol{\eta}+\mathbf{B}^{-1}\mathbf{C}\boldsymbol{\beta}
and
\mathbf{B}=N^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}^{0}\otimes\mathbf{I}_{T}
are defined as on page 1265 of
Bai 2009
. Note that for
\boldsymbol{\beta}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}}
, we may have

\displaystyle\sup_{\boldsymbol{\beta}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_{x}},\mathbf{F}\in\mathbb{D}_{F}}\left|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\prime}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\frac{2}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{F}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}\right|\neq o_{P}(1).

(A.2)

In our proof of consistency, we consider two cases; (i)
\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\beta}\|\leq C
and (ii)
\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\beta}\|>C
, where
C
is a large positive constant. Under (i),

\displaystyle\sup_{\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\beta}\|\leq C,\mathbf{F}\in\mathbb{D}_{F}}\left|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\prime}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\frac{2}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{F}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}\right|=O_{P}(N^{-1/2}\vee T^{-1/2})

(A.3)

by Lemma
A.2
. The expression given in (
A.7
) for
(NT)^{-1}[\mathrm{SSR}(\boldsymbol{\beta},F)-\mathrm{SSR}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0},\mathbf{F}^{0})]
therefore reduces to

\displaystyle(NT)^{-1}[\mathrm{SSR}(\boldsymbol{\beta},\mathbf{F})-\mathrm{SSR}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0},\mathbf{F}^{0})]

\displaystyle=\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\prime}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\mathbf{B}(\mathbf{F})\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\beta}+\boldsymbol{\theta}^{\prime}\mathbf{B}\boldsymbol{\theta}+\frac{2}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{F}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle+O_{P}(N^{-1/2}\vee T^{-1/2}),

(A.4)

where
\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\prime}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\mathbf{B}(\mathbf{F})\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\beta}
does not involve
\mathbf{F}^{0}
and
\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{F}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}
is independent of
\boldsymbol{\beta}
. Hence, provided
d_{max}\geq d_{f}
, the consistency of
\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}
in case (i) follows from the same arguments as in
Bai 2009
.

Under (ii), (
A.7
) can be written as

\displaystyle(NT)^{-1}[\mathrm{SSR}(\boldsymbol{\beta},\mathbf{F})-\mathrm{SSR}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0},\mathbf{F}^{0})]

\displaystyle=\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\prime}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\mathbf{B}(\mathbf{F})\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\beta}+\boldsymbol{\theta}^{\prime}\mathbf{B}\boldsymbol{\theta}+\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\prime}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\frac{2}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{F}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}+\frac{2}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{F}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle+O_{P}(N^{-1/2}\vee T^{-1/2})

\displaystyle\geq c_{0}\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\boldsymbol{\beta}\|^{2}+\boldsymbol{\beta}^{\prime}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\frac{2}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{F}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}+\boldsymbol{\theta}^{\prime}\mathbf{B}\boldsymbol{\theta}+\frac{2}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{F}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle+O_{P}(N^{-1/2}\vee T^{-1/2})

\displaystyle\geq\frac{c_{0}}{2}C^{2}+\boldsymbol{\theta}^{\prime}\mathbf{B}\boldsymbol{\theta}+\frac{2}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\mathbf{M}_{F}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}+O_{P}(N^{-1/2}\vee T^{-1/2}),

(A.5)

where
c_{0}
is defined in Assumption
2
, and the second inequality follows from the fact that the quadratic term dominates the linear one for large values of
C
. Hence,
(NT)^{-1}[\mathrm{SSR}(\boldsymbol{\beta},\mathbf{F})-\mathrm{SSR}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0},\mathbf{F}^{0})]>0
, but from the definition of
\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}
we also know that
\mathrm{SSR}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0},\widehat{\mathbf{F}})-\mathrm{SSR}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0},\mathbf{F}^{0})\leq 0
, which means that
\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}
cannot belong to (ii).

Note that since
\|(NT)^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{F}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}\|=O_{P}(N^{-1/2}\vee T^{-1/2})
by Lemma
A.2
, all we need is
C=C^{0}(N^{-1/2}\vee T^{-1/2})
for some large constant
C^{0}
in order to ensure that the last inequality of (
A.7
) holds. This implies
\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})=O_{P}(N^{-1/2}\vee T^{-1/2})
, so the proof is complete.
\blacksquare

Proof of Lemma
3.2
.

Suppose first that
d_{1}=0
, such that
d_{f}=0
. In this case,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\|\mathbf{y}_{i}-\mathbf{X}_{i}\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\|\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})+\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\|\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}\|^{2}+o_{P}(1).

(A.6)

This implies
\tau_{N}\asymp 1/\ln(T\vee N)
, which is much larger than
\widehat{\lambda}_{1,1}/\widehat{\lambda}_{1,0}
. The result then follows immediately.

Suppose now instead that
d_{1}>0
. Straightforward algebra reveals that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT^{\nu_{1}}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\|\mathbf{y}_{i}-\mathbf{X}_{i}\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT^{\nu_{1}}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\|\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})+\mathbf{F}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{i}^{0}+\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT^{\nu_{1}}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\|\mathbf{F}_{1}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{1,i}^{0}\|^{2}+o_{P}(1)

\displaystyle=(\mathrm{vec}\,\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\Gamma_{1}^{0}})^{\prime}\mathrm{vec}\,\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{F_{1}^{0}}+o_{P}(1),

(A.7)

which together with Assumption
1
implies
\tau_{N}\asymp 1/\ln(T\vee N)
. Note that for
d=1,\ldots,d_{1}
,

\displaystyle\frac{T^{\nu_{1}}}{\lambda_{1,d}}

\displaystyle=\frac{T^{2\nu_{1}}}{\mathbf{h}_{1,d}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{1}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{1}^{0}\mathbf{F}_{1}^{0}\mathbf{h}_{1,d}^{0}}=\frac{NT^{2\nu_{1}}}{\mathbf{h}_{1,d}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{1}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{1}^{0}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{1}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{1}^{0}\mathbf{F}_{1}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{1}^{0}\mathbf{h}_{1,d}^{0}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(T^{-\nu_{1}}\mathbf{h}_{1,d}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{1}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{1}^{0})(N^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{1}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{1}^{0})(T^{-\nu_{1}}\mathbf{F}_{1}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{1}^{0}\mathbf{h}_{1,d}^{0})}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(T^{-(\nu_{1}+\delta)/2}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{1,d}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{1}^{0})(N^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{1}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{1}^{0})(T^{-(\nu_{1}+\delta)/2}\mathbf{F}_{1}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{1,d}^{0})}(1+o_{P}(1))\asymp 1,

(A.8)

where the fourth equality follows from (
A.32
) and the last step is dues to (
A.28
) and Assumption
1
. Using Lemma
A.3
, (
A.7
) and (
A.7
), we obtain that

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{1,1}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{1,0}}

\displaystyle=\frac{T^{-\nu_{1}}\widehat{\lambda}_{1,1}}{(NT^{\nu_{1}})^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\|\mathbf{y}_{i}-\mathbf{X}_{i}\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}\|^{2}}\asymp 1,

(A.9)

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{1,d+1}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{1,d}}

\displaystyle=\frac{T^{-\nu_{1}}\widehat{\lambda}_{1,d+1}}{T^{-\nu_{1}}\widehat{\lambda}_{1,d}}\asymp 1

(A.10)

for
d=1,\ldots,d_{1}-1
, and
\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{1,d_{1}+1}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{1,d_{1}}}=O_{P}(T^{-(\nu_{1}-\nu_{2})})
. Moreover, for
d=d_{1}+1,\ldots,d_{max}
,
\frac{T^{-\nu_{1}}\widehat{\lambda}_{1,d}}{T^{-\nu_{1}}\widehat{\lambda}_{1,0}}=O_{P}(T^{-(\nu_{1}-\nu_{2})})
, which is less than
\tau_{N}
by (
A.7
) and Lemma
A.3
. The required result follows from this and the definition of
\widehat{d}_{1}
.
\blacksquare

Proof of Lemma
3.3
.

Each step in the sequential procedure of Step
2
introduces additional remainder terms that all converge to zero under Assumptions
3
and
5
by Lemmas
A.3
and
A.5
. This proves (a). Part (b) follows from the (rotational) consistency of
\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}
established as a part of the proofs of Lemmas
A.3
and
A.5
.
\blacksquare

Proof of Theorem
3.1
.

By Lemma
A.6
, the rate of convergence given in Lemma
3.1
is therefore not the best one possible. The improved rate implies that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\|=O_{P}((NT)^{-1/2}\vee\|\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})\|)=O_{P}((NT)^{-1/2}),

(A.11)

which can be inserted back into (
A.6
), leading to

\displaystyle\sqrt{NT}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\left[\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}+\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{\prime}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})\right)^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{X}_{i}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{B}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{-1}\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}-\sqrt{N}T^{-\nu_{G}/2}\mathbf{B}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{A}}_{1}+o_{P}(1).

(A.12)

Note that
\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})
is
\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{i}
with
\widehat{a}_{ij}
replaced by
a_{ij}
. We now show that the effect of the estimation of
a_{ij}
is negligible. We begin by noting how

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}[\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{\prime}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})\mathbf{D}_{T}-\mathbf{D}_{T}\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{i}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{i}\mathbf{D}_{T}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{D}_{T}\widehat{a}_{ij}-\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{D}_{T}a_{ij}.

(A.13)

Here,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{D}_{T}\widehat{a}_{ij}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{D}_{T}\sum_{g=1}^{G}(\mathbf{y}_{j}-\mathbf{X}_{j}\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{-g}\widehat{\boldsymbol{\gamma}}_{-g,j})^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}

\displaystyle\times(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}^{\prime}\widehat{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{g}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g})^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}^{\prime}(\mathbf{y}_{i}-\mathbf{X}_{i}\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}-\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{-g}\widehat{\boldsymbol{\gamma}}_{-g,i})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{D}_{T}\sum_{g=1}^{G}[\mathbf{X}_{j}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})+\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,j}^{0}+\mathbf{e}_{g,j}+\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}]^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}

\displaystyle\times T^{-\delta}\mathbf{V}_{g}^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}^{\prime}[\mathbf{X}_{j}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})+\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}+\mathbf{e}_{g,i}+\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{D}_{T}\sum_{g=1}^{G}[\mathbf{X}_{j}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})+\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,j}^{0}+\mathbf{e}_{g,j}+\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}]^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}

\displaystyle\times T^{-(\nu_{g}+\delta)}(T^{-\nu_{g}}\mathbf{V}_{g})^{-1}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}^{\prime}[\mathbf{X}_{j}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})+\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,i}^{0}+\mathbf{e}_{g,i}+\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}](1+o_{P}(1))

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{D}_{T}a_{ij}(1+o_{P}(1)),

(A.14)

where the second equality here follows from the definition of
\mathbf{V}_{g}^{0}
, which is again based on taking
d_{g}
as known, while the last equality follows from direct calculation. The effect of the estimation of
a_{ij}
in
\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{i}
is therefore negligible, which in turn implies that the right-hand side of (
A.7
) becomes

\displaystyle\sqrt{NT}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\left[\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}+\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{\prime}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})\right)^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{X}_{i}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\right]

\displaystyle=\sqrt{NT}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}\left[\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}+\left(\sum_{i=1}^{N}\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{i}^{\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{Z}}_{i}\right)^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\mathbf{X}_{i}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{1}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}\right]+o_{P}(1)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{NT}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})+o_{P}(1).

(A.15)

It follows that

\displaystyle\sqrt{NT}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})

\displaystyle=\mathbf{B}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{-1}\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}-\sqrt{N}T^{-\nu_{G}/2}\mathbf{B}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{A}}_{1}

\displaystyle+o_{P}(1).

(A.16)

Consider
(NT)^{-1/2}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}
. From the definition of
\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\mathbf{F})
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\mathbf{F}^{0})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}[\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})-\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\mathbf{F}^{0})]^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\mathbf{F}^{0})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}-\sqrt{NT}(\mathbf{R}_{1}-\mathbf{R}_{2}),

(A.17)

where

\displaystyle\mathbf{R}_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}(\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{F}}-\mathbf{P}_{F^{0}})\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i},

(A.18)

\displaystyle\mathbf{R}_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}a_{ij}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\prime}(\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{F}}-\mathbf{P}_{F^{0}})\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}.

(A.19)

Here,

\displaystyle\mathbf{R}_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT^{1+\delta}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}-\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H})\mathbf{H}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}+\frac{1}{NT^{1+\delta}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}-\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H})(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}-\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{NT^{1+\delta}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}-\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}+\frac{1}{NT^{1+\delta}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0}[\mathbf{H}\mathbf{H}^{\prime}-T^{\delta}(\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0})^{-1}]\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{R}_{11}+\mathbf{R}_{12}+\mathbf{R}_{13}+\mathbf{R}_{14},

(A.20)

with obvious implicit definitions of
\mathbf{R}_{11},\ldots,\mathbf{R}_{14}
and
\mathbf{H}=\mathrm{diag}(\mathbf{H}_{1},\ldots,\mathbf{H}_{G})
. Let
\mathbf{R}_{1m,j}
be the
j
-th row of
\mathbf{R}_{1m}
for
m\in\{1,\ldots,4\}
. In this notation,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{R}_{11,j}\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\frac{1}{NT^{\delta/2+1}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H})\otimes(T^{-\kappa_{j}/2}\mathbf{X}_{j,i})^{\prime}\right\|\|T^{-\delta/2}\mathrm{vec}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}-\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H})\|

\displaystyle=O_{P}((NT)^{-1/2})T^{-\delta/2}\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}-\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H}\|=o_{P}((NT)^{-1/2}),

(A.21)

where the equality follows from Lemma
A.2
and the fact that
T^{(\nu_{g}-\delta)/2}\mathbf{H}_{g}=O_{P}(1)
. Similarly, for
\mathbf{R}_{12}
,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{R}_{12,j}\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}^{\prime}\otimes(T^{-\kappa_{j}/2}\mathbf{X}_{j,i})^{\prime}\right\|\|T^{-\delta}\mathrm{vec}[(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}-\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H})(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}-\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H})^{\prime}]\|

\displaystyle=O_{P}(N^{-1/2})T^{-\delta}\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}-\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H}\|^{2}=o_{P}((NT)^{-1/2}),

(A.22)

where the second equality is due to (
A.54
).

Consider
\mathbf{R}_{14}
. From
T^{-\delta/2}\|\widehat{\mathbf{F}}-\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H}\|=o_{P}(1)
and
\|T^{(\nu_{g}-\delta)/2}\mathbf{H}_{g}\|=O_{P}(1)
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{I}_{d_{f}}-T^{\delta}(\mathbf{H}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle=o_{P}(1),

(A.23)

\displaystyle\|T^{\nu_{g}-\delta}\mathbf{H}_{g}\mathbf{H}_{g}^{\prime}-(T^{-\nu_{g}}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle=o_{P}(1).

(A.24)

Together with Lemma
A.2
this implies

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{R}_{14,j}\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{1}{NT^{1+\delta}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}T^{-\kappa_{j}/2}\mathbf{X}_{j,i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0}[\mathbf{H}\mathbf{H}^{\prime}-T^{\delta}\mathbf{H}(\mathbf{H}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H})^{-1}\mathbf{H}^{\prime}]\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}\right\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{1}{NT^{1+\delta}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}T^{-\kappa_{j}/2}\mathbf{X}_{j,i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H}[\mathbf{I}_{d_{f}}-T^{\delta}(\mathbf{H}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H})^{-1}]\mathbf{H}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\frac{1}{NT^{1+\delta}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}(\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H})\otimes(T^{-\kappa_{j}/2}\mathbf{X}_{j,i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H})\right\|\|\mathbf{I}_{d_{f}}-T^{\delta}(\mathbf{H}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle=O_{P}((NT)^{-1/2})\|\mathbf{I}_{d_{f}}-T^{\delta}(\mathbf{H}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H})^{-1}\|=o_{P}((NT)^{-1/2}).

(A.25)

Finally, let us consider
\mathbf{R}_{13}
.

\displaystyle\mathbf{R}_{13}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT^{1+\delta}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}-\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle=\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{NT^{1+\delta}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}\mathbf{H}_{g}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}-\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}\mathbf{H}_{g})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle=\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{NT^{1+\delta}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}\mathbf{H}_{g}\mathbf{H}_{g}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{H}_{g}^{-1}-\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}

(A.26)

Expanding
\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{H}_{g}^{-1}-\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}
as we did for
\mathbf{L}
above, we then just need to focus on the leading terms equivalent to
\mathbf{L}_{2}
,
\mathbf{L}_{8}
, and
\mathbf{L}_{10}
.

\displaystyle\mathbf{R}_{13}

\displaystyle=\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{NT^{1+\delta}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}\mathbf{H}_{g}\mathbf{H}_{g}^{\prime}\left(\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{X}_{j}(\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0})\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,j}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle+\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{NT^{1+\delta}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}\mathbf{H}_{g}\mathbf{H}_{g}^{\prime}\left(\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,j}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle+\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{NT^{1+\delta}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}\mathbf{H}_{g}\mathbf{H}_{g}^{\prime}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T^{\nu_{g}+1}}}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon}\frac{\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}}{T^{(\nu_{g}-1)/2}}\left(\frac{\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}}{T^{\nu_{g}}}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0}}{N}\right)^{-1}\right)^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle+o_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbf{R}_{131}+\mathbf{R}_{132}+\mathbf{R}_{133}+o_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right).

It is easy to see that
\|\mathbf{R}_{131}\|=o_{P}(\|\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0}-\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}_{0}\|)
, so negligible. We now examine
\mathbf{R}_{133}
. Then we can write

\displaystyle\mathbf{R}_{133}

\displaystyle=\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{NT^{1+\nu_{g}}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}\left(\frac{\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}}{T^{\nu_{g}}}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon}}{\sqrt{T^{\nu_{g}+1}}}\frac{\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}}{T^{(\nu_{g}-1)/2}}\left(\frac{\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}}{T^{\nu_{g}}}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0}}{N}\right)^{-1}\right)^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle=\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{T^{\nu_{g}+1}}\cdot\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\frac{\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}}{T^{(\nu_{g}-1)/2}}\left(\frac{\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}}{T^{\nu_{g}}}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0}}{N}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}}{T^{\nu_{g}}}\right)^{-1}\frac{\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0\prime}}{T^{(\nu_{g}-1)/2}}\boldsymbol{\Sigma}_{\varepsilon}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle=o_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\right),

(A.27)

where the first equality follows from (
A.24
), and the last step follows from some routine analysis using Assumption
3
. Below, we investigate
\mathbf{R}_{132}
, which is one source of the bias term.

\displaystyle\mathbf{R}_{132}

\displaystyle=\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{NT^{1+\delta}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}\mathbf{H}_{g}\mathbf{H}_{g}^{\prime}\left(\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}+\delta)/2}}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,j}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0\prime}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{g}\mathbf{Q}_{g}^{-1}\right)^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle=\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{NT^{1+\nu_{g}}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}(T^{\nu_{g}-\delta}\mathbf{H}_{g}\mathbf{H}_{g}^{\prime})\left(\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,j}^{0\prime}\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0}}{N}\right)^{-1}\right)^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle=\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{NT^{1+\nu_{g}}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}\left(\frac{\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}}{T^{\nu_{g}}}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{1}{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,j}^{0\prime}\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0}}{N}\right)^{-1}\right)^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}\cdot(1+o_{P}(1))

\displaystyle=\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{1}{NT^{(\nu_{g}-1)/2}}\cdot\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\frac{\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}}{T^{(\nu_{g}+1)/2}}\left(\frac{\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}}{T^{\nu_{g}}}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0}}{N}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,j}^{0}\frac{\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}}{T}\cdot(1+o_{P}(1)),

(A.28)

where the second equality follows from the definition of
\mathbf{Q}_{g}
, and the third equality follows from (
A.24
).
Then we are able to further write

\displaystyle\sqrt{NT}\mathbf{R}_{32}

\displaystyle=\sum_{g=1}^{G}\frac{T}{\sqrt{N}T^{\nu_{g}/2}}\cdot\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\frac{\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}}{T^{(\nu_{g}+1)/2}}\left(\frac{\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}}{T^{\nu_{g}}}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{g}^{0}}{N}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{g,j}^{0}\frac{\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}}{T}\cdot(1+o_{P}(1))

\displaystyle=\frac{T}{\sqrt{N}T^{\nu_{G}/2}}\cdot\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\frac{\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{G}^{0}}{T^{(\nu_{G}+1)/2}}\left(\frac{\mathbf{F}_{G}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{G}^{0}}{T^{\nu_{G}}}\right)^{-1}\left(\frac{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{G}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{G}^{0}}{N}\right)^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{G,j}^{0}\frac{\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}}{T}\cdot(1+o_{P}(1)).

(A.29)

Hence, by adding the results,

\displaystyle\sqrt{NT}\mathbf{R}_{1}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{NT}(\mathbf{R}_{11}+\mathbf{R}_{12}+\mathbf{R}_{13}+\mathbf{R}_{14})

\displaystyle=T^{(2-\nu_{G})/2}N^{-1/2}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}T^{-(\nu_{G}+1)/2}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{G}^{0}(T^{-\nu_{G}}\mathbf{F}_{G}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{G}^{0})^{-1}

\displaystyle\times(N^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{G}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{G}^{0})^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{G,j}^{0}T^{-1}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}+o_{P}(1).

(A.30)

\sqrt{NT}\mathbf{R}_{2}
can be evaluated in exactly the same way, and the limiting representation is analogous to the one given above for
\sqrt{NT}\mathbf{R}_{1}
with
\mathbf{X}_{i}
replaced by
-\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{X}_{j}a_{ij}
. Moreover,
\|\mathbf{B}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})-\mathbf{B}(\mathbf{F}^{0})\|=o_{P}(1).
It follows that if we define

\displaystyle\overline{\mathbf{A}}_{2}=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}T^{-(\nu_{G}+1)/2}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(0)^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{G}^{0}(T^{-\nu_{G}}\mathbf{F}_{G}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}_{G}^{0})^{-1}(N^{-1}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{G}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}_{G}^{0})^{-1}\boldsymbol{\gamma}_{G,j}^{0}T^{-1}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{j}^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i},

(A.31)

where
\mathbf{Z}_{i}(0)=\mathbf{X}_{i}-\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{X}_{j}a_{ij}
, then (
A.17
) reduces to

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\mathbf{F}^{0})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}-T^{(2-\nu_{G})/2}N^{-1/2}\overline{\mathbf{A}}_{2}+o_{P}(1),

(A.32)

which in turn implies that (
A.7
) becomes

\displaystyle\sqrt{NT}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})

\displaystyle=\mathbf{B}(\mathbf{F}^{0})^{-1}\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\mathbf{F}^{0})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}-\sqrt{N}T^{-\nu_{G}/2}\mathbf{B}(\mathbf{F}^{0})^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{A}}_{1}

\displaystyle-T^{(2-\nu_{G})/2}N^{-1/2}\mathbf{B}(\mathbf{F}^{0})^{-1}\overline{\mathbf{A}}_{2}+o_{P}(1).

(A.33)

The required result is now implied by Assumption
7
.
\blacksquare

Proof of Corollary
A.1
.

Under
NT^{-\nu_{G}}\to 0
, (
A.7
) in the proof of Theorem
3.1
may be written as

\displaystyle\sqrt{NT}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})=\mathbf{B}(\mathbf{F}^{0})^{-1}\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}+o_{P}(1).

Consider
(NT)^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}
, which we can write as

\displaystyle\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{\widehat{F}}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{F^{0}}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}-\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}(\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{F}}-\mathbf{P}_{F^{0}})\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{M}_{F^{0}}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}-\mathbf{R}_{1}.

(A.34)

As in the proof of Theorem
3.1
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{R}_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}(T^{-\delta}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}^{\prime}-\mathbf{P}_{F^{0}})\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{NT^{1+\delta}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}-\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H})\mathbf{H}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}+\frac{1}{NT^{1+\delta}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}-\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H})(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}-\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{NT^{1+\delta}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}-\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}+\frac{1}{NT^{1+\delta}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0}[\mathbf{H}\mathbf{H}^{\prime}-(\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0})^{-1}]\mathbf{F}^{0\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{R}_{11}+\mathbf{R}_{12}+\mathbf{R}_{13}+\mathbf{R}_{14},

(A.35)

where
\|\mathbf{R}_{11}\|
,
\|\mathbf{R}_{12}\|
and
\|\mathbf{R}_{14}\|
are all
o_{P}((NT)^{-1/2})
, just as before. Let us therefore consider
\mathbf{R}_{13}
, which is the source of the bias in the asymptotic distribution of
\sqrt{NT}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})
. The purpose of Assumption
A.1
is to control this term. Suppose first that condition (b) holds. Let
x_{j,k,t}
denote the
j
-th row of
\mathbf{x}_{k,t}
. In this notation,

\displaystyle E\left\|\frac{1}{NT^{\delta/2+1}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}^{\prime}\otimes(T^{-\kappa_{j}/2}\mathbf{X}_{j,i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}T^{-(\nu_{g}-\delta)/2})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N^{2}T^{\delta+2}}\sum_{s=1}^{T}E\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{N}\varepsilon_{i,s}\sum_{t=1}^{T}T^{-\kappa_{j}/2}x_{j,i,t}T^{-(\nu_{g}-1)/2}\mathbf{f}_{g,t}^{0\prime}T^{(\nu_{g}-1)/2}T^{-(\nu_{g}-\delta)/2}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(NT)^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{k=1}^{N}E\left[\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}T^{-\kappa_{j}/2}x_{j,i,t}T^{-(\nu_{g}-1)/2}\mathbf{f}_{g,t}^{0\prime}\right)\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}T^{-(\nu_{g}-1)/2}\mathbf{f}_{g,t}^{0}T^{-\kappa_{j}/2}x_{j,k,t}\right)\right]\sigma_{\varepsilon,ik}

\displaystyle=O(N^{-1}).

(A.36)

Moreover, by applying Assumption
7
(b) to the expression given for
\|T^{-\delta/2}\widehat{\mathbf{F}}_{2,d}-T^{-\nu_{2}/2}\mathbf{F}_{2}^{0}\mathbf{h}_{2,d}^{0}\|
in the proof of Lemma
A.5
, we can show that

\displaystyle\|T^{-\delta/2}\mathrm{vec}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}-\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H})^{\prime}\|=o_{P}(T^{-1/2}).

(A.37)

Making use of these results, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{R}_{3,j}\|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\frac{1}{NT^{\delta/2+1}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}^{\prime}\otimes(T^{-\kappa_{j}/2}\mathbf{X}_{j,i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H})\right\|\|T^{-\delta/2}\mathrm{vec}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}-\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H})^{\prime}\|

\displaystyle=o_{P}((NT)^{-1/2}).

(A.38)

Alternatively, we may invoke Assumption
7
(a) to arrive at the same result. In this case,
\|T^{-\delta/2}\mathrm{vec}(\widehat{\mathbf{F}}-\mathbf{F}^{0}\mathbf{H})^{\prime}\|=o_{P}(1)
, but we also have

\displaystyle E\left\|\frac{1}{NT^{\delta/2+1}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}^{\prime}\otimes(T^{-\kappa_{j}/2}\mathbf{X}_{j,i}^{\prime}\mathbf{F}_{g}^{0}T^{-(\nu_{g}-\delta)/2})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(NT)^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{k=1}^{N}E\left[\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}T^{-\kappa_{j}/2}x_{j,i,t}T^{-(\nu_{g}-1)/2}\mathbf{f}_{g,t}^{0\prime}\right)\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}T^{-(\nu_{g}-1)/2}\mathbf{f}_{g,t}^{0}T^{-\kappa_{j}/2}x_{j,k,t}\right)\right]\sigma_{\varepsilon,ik}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(NT)^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{k=1}^{N}E[T^{-\kappa_{j}}x_{j,i,t}x_{j,k,t}T^{-(\nu_{g}-1)}E(\|\mathbf{f}_{g,t}^{0}\|^{2}|\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{t})]\sigma_{\varepsilon,ik}

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{(NT)^{2}}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{s<t}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{k=1}^{N}E[T^{-\kappa_{j}}x_{j,i,t}x_{j,k,s}T^{-(\nu_{g}-1)}E(\mathbf{f}_{g,t}^{0\prime}\mathbf{f}_{g,s}^{0}|\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{t},\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{s})]\sigma_{\varepsilon,ik}

\displaystyle=O(1)\frac{1}{(NT)^{2}}\sum_{t=2}^{T}\sum_{s<t}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{k=1}^{N}|\phi_{ts}||\sigma_{\varepsilon,ik}|=O((NT)^{-1}),

(A.39)

and so
\|\mathbf{R}_{3,j}\|
is of the same order as before. The proof under Assumption
7
(c) is simpler and is therefore omitted.

Hence, by adding the results,

\displaystyle\sqrt{NT}\|\mathbf{R}_{1}\|\leq\sqrt{NT}(\|\mathbf{R}_{11}\|+\|\mathbf{R}_{12}\|+\|\mathbf{R}_{13}\|+\|\mathbf{R}_{14}\|)=o_{P}(1).

(A.40)

We have therefore shown that

\displaystyle\sqrt{NT}\left\|\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}(\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{F}}-\mathbf{P}_{F^{0}})\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}\right\|=o_{P}(1),

(A.41)

and we can similarly show that

\displaystyle\sqrt{NT}\left\|\frac{1}{NT}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\prime}(\mathbf{P}_{\widehat{F}}-\mathbf{P}_{F^{0}})\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{D}_{T}a_{ij}\right\|=o_{P}(1).

(A.42)

These results can be inserted into (
A.7
), giving

\displaystyle\sqrt{NT}\mathbf{D}_{T}^{-1}(\widehat{\boldsymbol{\beta}}-\boldsymbol{\beta}^{0})=\mathbf{B}(\mathbf{F}^{0})^{-1}\frac{1}{\sqrt{NT}}\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbf{D}_{T}\mathbf{Z}_{i}(\mathbf{F}^{0})^{\prime}\boldsymbol{\varepsilon}_{i}+o_{P}(1).

(A.43)

The sought result now follows from Assumption
7
.
\blacksquare
